    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall string_starts_with_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression value);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall string_substring_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression start, KRPC::Expression length);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall string_to_lower_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression arg);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall string_to_upper_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression arg);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall string_trim_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression arg);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall string_trim_end_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression arg);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall string_trim_start_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression arg);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall subtract_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall sum_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression arg);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall switch__call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression value, std::vector<std::vector<KRPC::Expression> > cases, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> bodies);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall take_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression count);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall throw__call(::krpc::Connection& connection, std::string service, std::string name, KRPC::Expression message);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall to_list_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression arg);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall to_set_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression arg);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall try_catch_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression body, std::string service, std::string name, std::optional<KRPC::Expression> message, KRPC::Expression handler);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall try_catch_all_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression body, std::optional<KRPC::Expression> message, KRPC::Expression handler);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall try_finally_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression body, KRPC::Expression finalizer);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall union__call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression arg1, KRPC::Expression arg2);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall variable_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, std::string name, KRPC::Type type);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall where_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression func);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall while__call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression condition, KRPC::Expression body);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall zip_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Expression arg1, KRPC::Expression arg2, KRPC::Expression func);

    ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall has_return_type_call();

    ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall parameter_types_call();

    ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall return_type_call();

    /**
     * The static methods of KRPC::Expression, bound to a connection.
     */
    class Bound {
     public:
      explicit Bound(const ::krpc::Connection& connection);

      /**
       * Numerical addition.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression add(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * The result of applying an accumulator function over a collection.
       *
       * Throws an exception if the collection is empty.
       * @return The accumulated value.
       * @param arg The collection.
       * @param func The accumulator function.
       */
      KRPC::Expression aggregate(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression func) const;

      /**
       * The result of applying an accumulator function over a collection, starting from a seed.
       *
       * The seed widens to the type of the accumulated value, the function's result.
       * @return The accumulated value.
       * @param arg The collection.
       * @param seed The seed value.
       * @param func The accumulator function.
       */
      KRPC::Expression aggregate_with_seed(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression seed, KRPC::Expression func) const;

      /**
       * Whether a predicate function returns true for every value of a collection.
       * @return Whether all items satisfy the predicate.
       * @param arg The collection.
       * @param predicate The predicate function.
       */
      KRPC::Expression all(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression predicate) const;

      /**
       * Logical or bitwise and, which evaluates both operands.
       *
       * Also takes two values of a flags enumeration, and gives the values they share.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression and_(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * Whether a predicate function returns true for any value of a collection.
       * @return Whether any item satisfies the predicate.
       * @param arg The collection.
       * @param predicate The predicate function.
       */
      KRPC::Expression any(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression predicate) const;

      /**
       * A statement adding a value to a list or a set.
       *
       * The value goes on the end of a list. A set holding the value is left unchanged.
       * @param collection The list or set to add to.
       * @param value The value to add.
       */
      KRPC::Expression append(KRPC::Expression collection, KRPC::Expression value) const;

      /**
       * An assignment to a local variable, state variable or function parameter.
       *
       * The value's type must be assignable to the variable's type. A numeric value of a
       * different type is widened, and narrowing it requires a cast.
       * @param variable The variable to assign to.
       * @param value The value to assign.
       */
      KRPC::Expression assign(KRPC::Expression variable, KRPC::Expression value) const;

      /**
       * The average of the values of a collection.
       *
       * Throws an exception if the collection is empty and its values are not nullable.
       * @return The average of the elements in the collection.
       * @param arg The list or set.
       */
      KRPC::Expression average(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * A block of statements, whose value is that of its last statement.
       * @param statements The statements.
       */
      KRPC::Expression block(std::vector<KRPC::Expression> statements) const;

      /**
       * A block of statements with local variables.
       *
       * The value of the block is that of its last statement. The variables are in scope
       * for the statements of the block, including within nested functions.
       * @param variables The local variables of the block.
       * @param statements The statements.
       */
      KRPC::Expression block_with_variables(std::vector<KRPC::Expression> variables, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> statements) const;

      /**
       * A break statement, which ends the enclosing loop or krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::switch_().
       */
      KRPC::Expression break_() const;

      /**
       * A dictionary built from a collection, with a key and a value computed from each
       * element.
       *
       * A repeated key takes the last of its values.
       * @return The dictionary.
       * @param arg The list or set.
       * @param key_func The function computing an element's key.
       * @param value_func The function computing an element's value.
       */
      KRPC::Expression build_dictionary(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression key_func, KRPC::Expression value_func) const;

      /**
       * An RPC call.
       *
       * The instance the call is made on, and the values of its arguments, are fixed
       * when the expression is created. A call to a procedure with no return value is
       * evaluated for its effects, as a statement in a krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::block(). A collection the
       * procedure returns is copied, as a client would receive it.
       * @param call The procedure call.
       */
      KRPC::Expression call(krpc::schema::ProcedureCall call) const;

      /**
       * An RPC call, where some or all of the arguments are computed by expressions.
       *
       * Position 0 is the instance of a class method or property. A position with no
       * expression takes the argument encoded in the call, or the parameter's default. A
       * collection the procedure returns is copied, as a client would receive it.
       * @param call The RPC to call.
       * @param args Expressions computing the call's arguments, by position.
       */
      KRPC::Expression call_with_arguments(krpc::schema::ProcedureCall call, std::map<int32_t, KRPC::Expression> args) const;

      /**
       * A value cast to the given type.
       *
       * A tuple or collection is cast element by element.
       * @param arg The value to cast.
       * @param type Type to cast the argument to.
       */
      KRPC::Expression cast(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Type type) const;

      /**
       * A statement removing every value from a list or a set, or every entry from a dictionary.
       * @param collection The list, set or dictionary.
       */
      KRPC::Expression clear(KRPC::Expression collection) const;

      /**
       * The values of one collection followed by those of another.
       *
       * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
       * @return The first collection followed by the second.
       * @param arg1 The first collection.
       * @param arg2 The second collection.
       */
      KRPC::Expression concat(KRPC::Expression arg1, KRPC::Expression arg2) const;

      /**
       * The second argument if the condition is true, and the third argument otherwise.
       *
       * Throws an exception if the two values have no common type.
       * @param condition The boolean condition.
       * @param if_true The value when the condition is true.
       * @param if_false The value when the condition is false.
       */
      KRPC::Expression conditional(KRPC::Expression condition, KRPC::Expression if_true, KRPC::Expression if_false) const;

      /**
       * Logical and, which evaluates the second operand only when the first is true.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression conditional_and(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * Logical or, which evaluates the second operand only when the first is false.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression conditional_or(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * A constant value of boolean type.
       * @param value The value.
       */
      KRPC::Expression constant_bool(bool value) const;

      /**
       * A constant value of bytes type.
       * @param value The value.
       */
      KRPC::Expression constant_bytes(std::string value) const;

      /**
       * A constant value of double precision floating point type.
       * @param value The value.
       */
      KRPC::Expression constant_double(double value) const;

      /**
       * A constant value of an enumeration a service defines.
       * @param service The name of the service the enumeration is defined in.
       * @param name The name of the enumeration.
       * @param value The value of the member of the enumeration.
       */
      KRPC::Expression constant_enum(std::string service, std::string name, int32_t value) const;

      /**
       * A constant value of single precision floating point type.
       * @param value The value.
       */
      KRPC::Expression constant_float(float value) const;

      /**
       * A constant value of integer type.
       * @param value The value.
       */
      KRPC::Expression constant_int(int32_t value) const;

      /**
       * A constant value of long integer type.
       * @param value The value.
       */
      KRPC::Expression constant_long(int64_t value) const;

      /**
       * A null constant of the given type.
       *
       * The type is made nullable, as by krpc::services::KRPC::Type::nullable_type().
       * @param type The type of the value.
       */
      KRPC::Expression constant_null(KRPC::Type type) const;

      /**
       * A constant object, an instance of a class a service defines.
       *
       * The object is given by its object identifier.
       * @param value The object identifier of the object.
       */
      KRPC::Expression constant_object(uint64_t value) const;

      /**
       * A constant value of string type.
       * @param value The value.
       */
      KRPC::Expression constant_string(std::string value) const;

      /**
       * A constant value of unsigned integer type.
       * @param value The value.
       */
      KRPC::Expression constant_u_int(uint32_t value) const;

      /**
       * A constant value of unsigned long integer type.
       * @param value The value.
       */
      KRPC::Expression constant_u_long(uint64_t value) const;

      /**
       * Whether a collection contains a value.
       * @return Whether the collection contains the value.
       * @param arg The collection.
       * @param value The value to look for.
       */
      KRPC::Expression contains(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression value) const;

      /**
       * Whether a dictionary contains a key.
       * @return Whether the dictionary contains the key.
       * @param dictionary The dictionary.
       * @param key The key to look for.
       */
      KRPC::Expression contains_key(KRPC::Expression dictionary, KRPC::Expression key) const;

      /**
       * A continue statement, which skips to the next iteration of the enclosing loop.
       */
      KRPC::Expression continue_() const;

      /**
       * The string representation of a value.
       *
       * A value other than a number, boolean, string or enumeration converts as .NET
       * formats it, which for a collection is the name of its type. Throws an exception if
       * the value is null.
       * @return The string representation of the value.
       * @param arg The value to convert.
       */
      KRPC::Expression convert_to_string(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * The number of values in a collection.
       * @return The number of elements in the collection.
       * @param arg The list, set or dictionary.
       */
      KRPC::Expression count(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * A dictionary of the given keys and corresponding values, with numbers widened to
       * their common type. Throws an exception if there are no keys, the counts differ, the
       * keys are not of a valid key type, or a key is null.
       *
       * A repeated key takes the last of its values.
       * @return The dictionary.
       * @param keys The keys.
       * @param values The values.
       */
      KRPC::Expression create_dictionary(std::vector<KRPC::Expression> keys, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> values) const;

      /**
       * An empty dictionary of the given key and value types. Throws an exception if the
       * key type is nullable or not a valid key type.
       * @return The empty dictionary.
       * @param key_type The type of the dictionary's keys.
       * @param value_type The type of the dictionary's values.
       */
      KRPC::Expression create_empty_dictionary(KRPC::Type key_type, KRPC::Type value_type) const;

      /**
       * An empty list of the given value type.
       * @return The empty list.
       * @param value_type The type of the values the list holds.
       */
      KRPC::Expression create_empty_list(KRPC::Type value_type) const;

      /**
       * An empty set of the given value type. Throws an exception if the value type is
       * nullable.
       * @return The empty set.
       * @param value_type The type of the values the set holds.
       */
      KRPC::Expression create_empty_set(KRPC::Type value_type) const;

      /**
       * A list of the given values, with numbers widened to their common type. Throws an
       * exception if there are no values.
       * @return The list.
       * @param values The values.
       */
      KRPC::Expression create_list(std::vector<KRPC::Expression> values) const;

      /**
       * A set of the given values, with numbers widened to their common type. Throws an
       * exception if there are no values, or a value is null.
       *
       * The values are evaluated in an unspecified order.
       * @return The set.
       * @param values The values.
       */
      KRPC::Expression create_set(std::set<KRPC::Expression> values) const;

      /**
       * A structure with the given field values. Throws an exception if the field values do
       * not match its fields.
       * @return The structure.
       * @param type The type of the structure.
       * @param field_values The values of the structure's fields,
       * in the order the structure declares them.
       */
      KRPC::Expression create_struct(KRPC::Type type, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> field_values) const;

      /**
       * A tuple of the given elements. Throws an exception if there are no elements or more
       * than seven.
       * @return The tuple.
       * @param elements The elements.
       */
      KRPC::Expression create_tuple(std::vector<KRPC::Expression> elements) const;

      /**
       * The keys of a dictionary, as a list.
       *
       * The collection operations take the keys of a dictionary from here, and its values
       * from krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::dictionary_values().
       * @return The keys of the dictionary.
       * @param dictionary The dictionary.
       */
      KRPC::Expression dictionary_keys(KRPC::Expression dictionary) const;

      /**
       * The values of a dictionary, as a list.
       * @return The values of the dictionary.
       * @param dictionary The dictionary.
       */
      KRPC::Expression dictionary_values(KRPC::Expression dictionary) const;

      /**
       * The values of a collection with duplicates removed, keeping the first
       * occurrence of each.
       *
       * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
       * @return The distinct values of the collection.
       * @param arg The collection.
       */
      KRPC::Expression distinct(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * Numerical division.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression divide(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * The value at the given position in a collection.
       *
       * Throws an exception if the index is out of range.
       * @return The value at the given position.
       * @param arg The collection.
       * @param index The position of the value, counting from zero.
       */
      KRPC::Expression element_at(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression index) const;

      /**
       * Equality comparison.
       *
       * Collections, tuples, bytes and structures compare by their contents. Throws an
       * exception if the operands are of unrelated types.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression equal(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * The values of the first collection that are not in the second, with
       * duplicates removed.
       *
       * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
       * @return The difference between the two collections.
       * @param arg1 The first collection.
       * @param arg2 The second collection.
       */
      KRPC::Expression except(KRPC::Expression arg1, KRPC::Expression arg2) const;

      /**
       * Logical or bitwise exclusive or.
       *
       * Also takes two values of a flags enumeration, and gives the values in only one of them.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression exclusive_or(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * The first value of a collection.
       *
       * Throws an exception if the collection is empty.
       * @return The first value.
       * @param arg The collection.
       */
      KRPC::Expression first(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * A loop that evaluates the body once for each value of a collection.
       *
       * The loop variable is a local variable declared by an enclosing
       * krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::block_with_variables(), or a parameter of an enclosing krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::lambda().
       * A krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::break_() or krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::continue_() in the body applies to this loop.
       * @param variable The loop variable.
       * @param collection The collection to iterate over.
       * @param body The statement evaluated on each iteration.
       */
      KRPC::Expression for_each(KRPC::Expression variable, KRPC::Expression collection, KRPC::Expression body) const;

      /**
       * An element of a tuple, list or dictionary.
       *
       * A tuple takes a constant integer index, a list a zero indexed integer and a
       * dictionary a key. Throws an exception if the index is out of range or the key is
       * missing, and for a tuple when the expression is created.
       * @return The element.
       * @param arg The tuple, list or dictionary.
       * @param index The index or key of the element.
       */
      KRPC::Expression get(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression index) const;

      /**
       * The value of a field of a structure. Throws an exception if the value is not a
       * structure, or has no field of the given name.
       * @return The value of the field.
       * @param arg The structure.
       * @param name The name of the field to access.
       */
      KRPC::Expression get_field(KRPC::Expression arg, std::string name) const;

      /**
       * Greater than numerical comparison.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression greater_than(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * Greater than or equal numerical comparison.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression greater_than_or_equal(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * The values of a collection grouped by the key a function produces for each of them.
       * @return A dictionary of each key to the values that produced it.
       * @param arg The collection.
       * @param key A function producing the key to group values by.
       */
      KRPC::Expression group_by(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression key) const;

      /**
       * An if statement, with no value.
       *
       * Use krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::conditional() for an if-then-else that produces a value.
       * @param condition The boolean condition.
       * @param body The statement evaluated when the condition is true.
       */
      KRPC::Expression if_then(KRPC::Expression condition, KRPC::Expression body) const;

      /**
       * An if-else statement, with no value.
       *
       * Use krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::conditional() for an if-then-else that produces a value.
       * @param condition The boolean condition.
       * @param body The statement evaluated when the condition is true.
       * @param else_body The statement evaluated when the condition is false.
       */
      KRPC::Expression if_then_else(KRPC::Expression condition, KRPC::Expression body, KRPC::Expression else_body) const;

      /**
       * The values in both of two collections, with duplicates removed.
       *
       * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
       * @return The intersection of the two collections.
       * @param arg1 The first collection.
       * @param arg2 The second collection.
       */
      KRPC::Expression intersect(KRPC::Expression arg1, KRPC::Expression arg2) const;

      /**
       * A function call.
       * @return A function call.
       * @param function The function to call.
       * @param args The arguments to call the function with.
       */
      KRPC::Expression invoke(KRPC::Expression function, std::map<std::string, KRPC::Expression> args) const;

      /**
       * Whether a value is null. Throws an exception if the type of the value cannot be null.
       * @param value The value.
       */
      KRPC::Expression is_null(KRPC::Expression value) const;

      /**
       * A function with the given parameters and body.
       *
       * The body is an expression or a block of statements, and a body with no value is
       * evaluated for its effects. A krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::return_() or krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::return_nothing()
       * statement ends the evaluation.
       * @return A function.
       * @param parameters The parameters of the function.
       * @param body The body of the function.
       */
      KRPC::Expression lambda(std::vector<KRPC::Expression> parameters, KRPC::Expression body) const;

      /**
       * The last value of a collection.
       *
       * Throws an exception if the collection is empty.
       * @return The last value.
       * @param arg The collection.
       */
      KRPC::Expression last(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * Bitwise left shift.
       *
       * Only the low 5 bits of the count are used for a 32-bit value, and the low 6 bits
       * for a 64-bit value.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression left_shift(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * Less than numerical comparison.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression less_than(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * Less than or equal numerical comparison.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression less_than_or_equal(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * A match statement, which evaluates the first matching case.
       *
       * Test values compare as for krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::equal(), and a case with none is the default.
       * A krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::break_() in a body ends the enclosing loop.
       * @param value The value to match.
       * @param cases The test values of each case.
       * @param bodies The statement of each case.
       */
      KRPC::Expression match(KRPC::Expression value, std::vector<std::vector<KRPC::Expression> > cases, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> bodies) const;

      /**
       * The largest value of a collection.
       *
       * Throws an exception if the collection is empty and its values are not nullable.
       * @return The largest value in the collection.
       * @param arg The list or set.
       */
      KRPC::Expression max(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * The value of a collection with the largest key.
       *
       * Throws an exception if the collection is empty.
       * @return The value with the largest key.
       * @param arg The collection.
       * @param key A function producing the key to compare values by.
       */
      KRPC::Expression max_by(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression key) const;

      /**
       * The smallest value of a collection.
       *
       * Throws an exception if the collection is empty and its values are not nullable.
       * @return The smallest value in the collection.
       * @param arg The list or set.
       */
      KRPC::Expression min(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * The value of a collection with the smallest key.
       *
       * Throws an exception if the collection is empty.
       * @return The value with the smallest key.
       * @param arg The collection.
       * @param key A function producing the key to compare values by.
       */
      KRPC::Expression min_by(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression key) const;

      /**
       * Numerical modulo operator.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       * @return The remainder of arg0 divided by arg1
       */
      KRPC::Expression modulo(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * Numerical multiplication.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression multiply(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * Numerical negation, which negates a uint as a long.
       * @param arg The operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression negate(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * Logical or bitwise negation.
       *
       * Also takes a value of a flags enumeration, and gives the declared values it does not hold.
       * @param arg The operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression not_(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * Inequality comparison.
       *
       * Collections, tuples, bytes and structures compare by their contents. Throws an
       * exception if the operands are of unrelated types.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression not_equal(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * Logical or bitwise or, which evaluates both operands.
       *
       * Also takes two values of a flags enumeration, and gives their combination.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression or_(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * The values of a collection ordered by the key a function produces for each of them.
       *
       * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
       * @return The ordered collection.
       * @param arg The collection to order.
       * @param key A function producing the key to order values by.
       */
      KRPC::Expression order_by(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression key) const;

      /**
       * A named parameter of a function.
       *
       * A value is bound to it by name with krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::invoke(), or by its position in
       * the parameters of a krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::lambda().
       * @return A named parameter.
       * @param name The name of the parameter.
       * @param type The type of the parameter.
       */
      KRPC::Expression parameter(std::string name, KRPC::Type type) const;

      /**
       * Numerical power operator.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       * @return arg0 raised to the power of arg1, at the common type of the operands
       */
      KRPC::Expression power(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * A removal of a value from a list or a set, or of an entry from a dictionary.
       *
       * The first occurrence is removed from a list.
       * @return Whether the collection held the value.
       * @param collection The list, set or dictionary.
       * @param value The value to remove, or the key of the entry to
       * remove.
       */
      KRPC::Expression remove(KRPC::Expression collection, KRPC::Expression value) const;

      /**
       * A statement removing the element at an index of a list.
       *
       * Throws an exception if the index is out of range.
       * @param list The list.
       * @param index The index of the element, counting from zero.
       */
      KRPC::Expression remove_at(KRPC::Expression list, KRPC::Expression index) const;

      /**
       * A return statement, which ends the enclosing function with a value.
       * @param value The value to return.
       */
      KRPC::Expression return_(KRPC::Expression value) const;

      /**
       * A return statement, which ends the enclosing function with no value.
       */
      KRPC::Expression return_nothing() const;

      /**
       * The values of a collection in the opposite order.
       *
       * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
       * @return The reversed collection.
       * @param arg The collection.
       */
      KRPC::Expression reverse(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * Bitwise right shift.
       *
       * Only the low 5 bits of the count are used for a 32-bit value, and the low 6 bits
       * for a 64-bit value.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression right_shift(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * The result of a function for each value of a collection.
       *
       * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
       * @return The collection of function results.
       * @param arg The list or set.
       * @param func The function, taking an element of the collection.
       */
      KRPC::Expression select(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression func) const;

      /**
       * The values of the collections a function returns for each value of a collection,
       * flattened into one.
       *
       * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
       * @return The flattened collection of function results.
       * @param arg The list or set.
       * @param func The function, taking an element of the collection
       * and returning a collection.
       */
      KRPC::Expression select_many(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression func) const;

      /**
       * A statement setting an element of a list, or an entry of a dictionary.
       *
       * A key the dictionary does not hold is added. Throws an exception if the index
       * is out of range for the list.
       * @param collection The list or dictionary.
       * @param index The index of the element, counting from zero, or the key of the entry.
       * @param value The value to set.
       */
      KRPC::Expression set(KRPC::Expression collection, KRPC::Expression index, KRPC::Expression value) const;

      /**
       * A collection without its first count values.
       *
       * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
       * @return The collection without its first count values.
       * @param arg The collection.
       * @param count The number of values to skip.
       */
      KRPC::Expression skip(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression count) const;

      /**
       * A variable that keeps its value between evaluations of a function.
       *
       * The initial value is evaluated once, when the variable is created, and gives the
       * variable its type. The value belongs to the variable, so every function that
       * contains it shares it.
       * @return A state variable.
       * @param name The name of the variable.
       * @param initial_value The initial value of the variable.
       */
      KRPC::Expression state_variable(std::string name, KRPC::Expression initial_value) const;

      /**
       * The concatenation of strings.
       *
       * Use krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::convert_to_string() to convert other values to strings.
       * @return The concatenated string.
       * @param args The strings to concatenate, in order.
       */
      KRPC::Expression string_concat(std::vector<KRPC::Expression> args) const;

      /**
       * Whether one string occurs within another.
       * @return Whether the value occurs in the string.
       * @param arg The string to search.
       * @param value The string to search for.
       */
      KRPC::Expression string_contains(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression value) const;

      /**
       * Whether a string ends with another.
       * @return Whether the string ends with the value.
       * @param arg The string to test.
       * @param value The string to test for.
       */
      KRPC::Expression string_ends_with(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression value) const;

      /**
       * The character at the given position in a string, as a string of length one.
       *
       * Throws an exception if the index is out of range.
       * @return The character at the given position.
       * @param arg The string.
       * @param index The position of the character, counting from zero.
       */
      KRPC::Expression string_get(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression index) const;

      /**
       * The position of the first occurrence of one string within another.
       * @return The position of the value, counting from zero, or -1 if it does not occur.
       * @param arg The string to search.
       * @param value The string to search for.
       */
      KRPC::Expression string_index_of(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression value) const;

      /**
       * Strings joined together, separated by another string.
       * @return The joined string.
       * @param separator The string to put between the values.
       * @param values The strings to join.
       */
      KRPC::Expression string_join(KRPC::Expression separator, KRPC::Expression values) const;

      /**
       * The number of characters in a string.
       * @return The length of the string.
       * @param arg The string.
       */
      KRPC::Expression string_length(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * A string with every occurrence of one string replaced by another.
       *
       * Throws an exception if the string to replace is empty.
       * @return The string with the replacements made.
       * @param arg The string to search.
       * @param old_value The string to replace.
       * @param new_value The string to replace it with.
       */
      KRPC::Expression string_replace(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression old_value, KRPC::Expression new_value) const;

      /**
       * The parts of a string separated by another string.
       * @return The parts of the string.
       * @param arg The string to split.
       * @param separator The string that separates the parts.
       */
      KRPC::Expression string_split(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression separator) const;

      /**
       * Whether a string starts with another.
       * @return Whether the string starts with the value.
       * @param arg The string to test.
       * @param value The string to test for.
       */
      KRPC::Expression string_starts_with(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression value) const;

      /**
       * Part of a string.
       *
       * Throws an exception if the start or the length is out of range.
       * @return The substring.
       * @param arg The string.
       * @param start The position to start at, counting from zero.
       * @param length The number of characters to take.
       */
      KRPC::Expression string_substring(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression start, KRPC::Expression length) const;

      /**
       * A string in lower case, independently of the game's language.
       * @return The string in lower case.
       * @param arg The string.
       */
      KRPC::Expression string_to_lower(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * A string in upper case, independently of the game's language.
       * @return The string in upper case.
       * @param arg The string.
       */
      KRPC::Expression string_to_upper(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * A string with white space removed from both ends.
       * @return The trimmed string.
       * @param arg The string.
       */
      KRPC::Expression string_trim(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * A string with white space removed from its end.
       * @return The trimmed string.
       * @param arg The string.
       */
      KRPC::Expression string_trim_end(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * A string with white space removed from its start.
       * @return The trimmed string.
       * @param arg The string.
       */
      KRPC::Expression string_trim_start(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * Numerical subtraction.
       * @param arg0 The first operand.
       * @param arg1 The second operand.
       */
      KRPC::Expression subtract(KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1) const;

      /**
       * The sum of the values of a collection.
       * @return The sum of the elements in the collection.
       * @param arg The list or set.
       */
      KRPC::Expression sum(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * A switch statement, which evaluates from the first matching case on.
       *
       * Test values compare as for krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::equal(), and a case with none is the default.
       * A krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::break_() in a body ends the switch.
       * @param value The value to match.
       * @param cases The test values of each case.
       * @param bodies The statement of each case.
       */
      KRPC::Expression switch_(KRPC::Expression value, std::vector<std::vector<KRPC::Expression> > cases, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> bodies) const;

      /**
       * The first count values of a collection.
       *
       * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
       * @return The first count values of the collection.
       * @param arg The collection.
       * @param count The number of values to take.
       */
      KRPC::Expression take(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression count) const;

      /**
       * A throw statement, which raises the named exception.
       *
       * The client receives the exception as an error from the function, or from the stream
       * or event evaluating it. For example, the service `KRPC` and the name
       * `Error` raise krpc::services::KRPC::Error. Throws an exception if the service does not
       * declare it.
       * @param service The name of the service the exception is defined in.
       * @param name The name of the exception.
       * @param message The message of the exception.
       */
      KRPC::Expression throw_(std::string service, std::string name, KRPC::Expression message) const;

      /**
       * A collection as a list.
       * @return The collection as a list.
       * @param arg The collection.
       */
      KRPC::Expression to_list(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * A collection as a set.
       *
       * Throws an exception if a value is null.
       * @return The collection as a set.
       * @param arg The collection.
       */
      KRPC::Expression to_set(KRPC::Expression arg) const;

      /**
       * A try-catch statement that handles the named exception.
       *
       * Only an exception a service declares can be named, and krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::try_catch_all()
       * handles any exception. Throws an exception if the service does not declare it.
       * @param body The statement to evaluate.
       * @param service The name of the service the exception is defined in.
       * @param name The name of the exception.
       * @param message A string variable assigned the exception's message, or null.
       * @param handler The statement evaluated when the exception is caught.
       */
      KRPC::Expression try_catch(KRPC::Expression body, std::string service, std::string name, std::optional<KRPC::Expression> message, KRPC::Expression handler) const;

      /**
       * A try-catch statement that handles any exception.
       * @param body The statement to evaluate.
       * @param message A string variable assigned the exception's message, or null.
       * @param handler The statement evaluated when an exception is caught.
       */
      KRPC::Expression try_catch_all(KRPC::Expression body, std::optional<KRPC::Expression> message, KRPC::Expression handler) const;

      /**
       * A try-finally statement.
       *
       * The finalizer is evaluated after the body, whether or not the body raises an
       * exception. A break, continue or return cannot leave the finalizer.
       * @param body The statement to evaluate.
       * @param finalizer The statement evaluated after the body.
       */
      KRPC::Expression try_finally(KRPC::Expression body, KRPC::Expression finalizer) const;

      /**
       * The values in either of two collections, with duplicates removed.
       *
       * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
       * @return The union of the two collections.
       * @param arg1 The first collection.
       * @param arg2 The second collection.
       */
      KRPC::Expression union_(KRPC::Expression arg1, KRPC::Expression arg2) const;

      /**
       * A local variable, declared by krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::block_with_variables().
       * @return A local variable.
       * @param name The name of the variable.
       * @param type The type of the variable.
       */
      KRPC::Expression variable(std::string name, KRPC::Type type) const;

      /**
       * The values of a collection for which a predicate function returns true.
       *
       * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
       * @return The filtered collection.
       * @param arg The list or set.
       * @param func The predicate function, taking an element of the collection.
       */
      KRPC::Expression where(KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression func) const;

      /**
       * A while loop, which evaluates the body while the condition is true.
       *
       * A krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::break_() or krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::continue_() in the body applies to this loop.
       * @param condition The boolean condition.
       * @param body The statement evaluated on each iteration.
       */
      KRPC::Expression while_(KRPC::Expression condition, KRPC::Expression body) const;

      /**
       * The results of a function applied to the values of two collections in pairs.
       *
       * The result is as long as the shorter of the two collections. It is lazily
       * evaluated, and krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
       * @return The combined collection.
       * @param arg1 The first collection.
       * @param arg2 The second collection.
       * @param func A function combining a value from each collection.
       */
      KRPC::Expression zip(KRPC::Expression arg1, KRPC::Expression arg2, KRPC::Expression func) const;

     private:
      mutable ::krpc::Connection _connection;
    };

    /**
     * Binds the static methods of KRPC::Expression to a connection.
     * @param connection The connection the methods are called on.
     * @return The bound methods.
     */
    static Bound bind(const ::krpc::Connection& connection);
  };

  /**
   * The type of a value a procedure takes or returns.
   */
  class Type : public ::krpc::RemoteObject<Type> {
   public:
    Type();
    Type(const ::krpc::Connection& connection, uint64_t id);

    /**
     * A boolean type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type bool_(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    /**
     * A byte sequence type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type bytes(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    /**
     * The type of a class defined by a service.
     * @param connection A connection object.
     * @param service The name of the service the class is defined in.
     * @param name The name of the class.
     */
    static KRPC::Type class_type(::krpc::Connection& connection, std::string service, std::string name);

    /**
     * A dictionary type. Throws an exception if the key type is nullable or not a valid
     * key type.
     * @param connection A connection object.
     * @param key_type The type of the keys of the dictionary.
     * @param value_type The type of the values of the dictionary.
     */
    static KRPC::Type dictionary_type(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Type key_type, KRPC::Type value_type);

    /**
     * A double precision floating point number type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type double_(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    /**
     * The type of an enumeration defined by a service.
     * @param connection A connection object.
     * @param service The name of the service the enumeration is defined in.
     * @param name The name of the enumeration.
     */
    static KRPC::Type enumeration_type(::krpc::Connection& connection, std::string service, std::string name);

    /**
     * A single precision floating point number type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type float_(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    /**
     * A signed 32-bit integer type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type int_(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    /**
     * A list type.
     * @param connection A connection object.
     * @param value_type The type of the elements of the list.
     */
    static KRPC::Type list_type(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Type value_type);

    /**
     * A signed 64-bit integer type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type long_(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    /**
     * A nullable type, holding a value of the given type or null.
     * @param connection A connection object.
     * @param value_type The type of the value.
     */
    static KRPC::Type nullable_type(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Type value_type);

    /**
     * A set type. Throws an exception if the element type is nullable.
     * @param connection A connection object.
     * @param value_type The type of the elements of the set.
     */
    static KRPC::Type set_type(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Type value_type);

    /**
     * A string type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type string(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    /**
     * The type of a structure defined by a service.
     * @param connection A connection object.
     * @param service The name of the service the structure is defined in.
     * @param name The name of the structure.
     */
    static KRPC::Type struct_type(::krpc::Connection& connection, std::string service, std::string name);

    /**
     * A tuple type. Throws an exception if there are no element types or more than seven.
     * @param connection A connection object.
     * @param value_types The types of the elements of the tuple.
     */
    static KRPC::Type tuple_type(::krpc::Connection& connection, std::vector<KRPC::Type> value_types);

    /**
     * An unsigned 32-bit integer type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type u_int(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    /**
     * An unsigned 64-bit integer type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type u_long(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    /**
     * The kind of the type.
     */
    KRPC::TypeCode code();

    /**
     * The name of a class, enumeration or structure type, or an empty string.
     */
    std::string name();

    /**
     * Whether a value of the type may be null.
     *
     * Any type can be nullable. A string or class value of a type that is not nullable
     * is never null.
     */
    bool nullable();

    /**
     * The service a class, enumeration or structure type is defined in, or an empty string.
     */
    std::string service();

    /**
     * The types of the elements of a tuple, list, set or dictionary type.
     *
     * The value types of a tuple, the value type of a list or set, or the key and value
     * types of a dictionary. An empty list for any other type.
     */
    std::vector<KRPC::Type> types();

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> bool__stream(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> bytes_stream(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> class_type_stream(::krpc::Connection& connection, std::string service, std::string name);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> dictionary_type_stream(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Type key_type, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> double__stream(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> enumeration_type_stream(::krpc::Connection& connection, std::string service, std::string name);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> float__stream(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> int__stream(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> list_type_stream(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> long__stream(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> nullable_type_stream(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> set_type_stream(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> string_stream(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> struct_type_stream(::krpc::Connection& connection, std::string service, std::string name);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> tuple_type_stream(::krpc::Connection& connection, std::vector<KRPC::Type> value_types);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> u_int_stream(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> u_long_stream(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::TypeCode> code_stream();

    ::krpc::Stream<std::string> name_stream();

    ::krpc::Stream<bool> nullable_stream();

    ::krpc::Stream<std::string> service_stream();

    ::krpc::Stream<std::vector<KRPC::Type>> types_stream();

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall bool__call(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall bytes_call(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall class_type_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, std::string service, std::string name);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall dictionary_type_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Type key_type, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall double__call(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall enumeration_type_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, std::string service, std::string name);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall float__call(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall int__call(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall list_type_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall long__call(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall nullable_type_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall set_type_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall string_call(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall struct_type_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, std::string service, std::string name);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall tuple_type_call(::krpc::Connection& connection, std::vector<KRPC::Type> value_types);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall u_int_call(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall u_long_call(::krpc::Connection& connection);

    ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall code_call();

    ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall name_call();

    ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall nullable_call();

    ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall service_call();

    ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall types_call();

    /**
     * The static methods of KRPC::Type, bound to a connection.
     */
    class Bound {
     public:
      explicit Bound(const ::krpc::Connection& connection);

      /**
       * A boolean type.
       */
      KRPC::Type bool_() const;

      /**
       * A byte sequence type.
       */
      KRPC::Type bytes() const;

      /**
       * The type of a class defined by a service.
       * @param service The name of the service the class is defined in.
       * @param name The name of the class.
       */
      KRPC::Type class_type(std::string service, std::string name) const;

      /**
       * A dictionary type. Throws an exception if the key type is nullable or not a valid
       * key type.
       * @param key_type The type of the keys of the dictionary.
       * @param value_type The type of the values of the dictionary.
       */
      KRPC::Type dictionary_type(KRPC::Type key_type, KRPC::Type value_type) const;

      /**
       * A double precision floating point number type.
       */
      KRPC::Type double_() const;

      /**
       * The type of an enumeration defined by a service.
       * @param service The name of the service the enumeration is defined in.
       * @param name The name of the enumeration.
       */
      KRPC::Type enumeration_type(std::string service, std::string name) const;

      /**
       * A single precision floating point number type.
       */
      KRPC::Type float_() const;

      /**
       * A signed 32-bit integer type.
       */
      KRPC::Type int_() const;

      /**
       * A list type.
       * @param value_type The type of the elements of the list.
       */
      KRPC::Type list_type(KRPC::Type value_type) const;

      /**
       * A signed 64-bit integer type.
       */
      KRPC::Type long_() const;

      /**
       * A nullable type, holding a value of the given type or null.
       * @param value_type The type of the value.
       */
      KRPC::Type nullable_type(KRPC::Type value_type) const;

      /**
       * A set type. Throws an exception if the element type is nullable.
       * @param value_type The type of the elements of the set.
       */
      KRPC::Type set_type(KRPC::Type value_type) const;

      /**
       * A string type.
       */
      KRPC::Type string() const;

      /**
       * The type of a structure defined by a service.
       * @param service The name of the service the structure is defined in.
       * @param name The name of the structure.
       */
      KRPC::Type struct_type(std::string service, std::string name) const;

      /**
       * A tuple type. Throws an exception if there are no element types or more than seven.
       * @param value_types The types of the elements of the tuple.
       */
      KRPC::Type tuple_type(std::vector<KRPC::Type> value_types) const;

      /**
       * An unsigned 32-bit integer type.
       */
      KRPC::Type u_int() const;

      /**
       * An unsigned 64-bit integer type.
       */
      KRPC::Type u_long() const;

     private:
      mutable ::krpc::Connection _connection;
    };

    /**
     * Binds the static methods of KRPC::Type to a connection.
     * @param connection The connection the methods are called on.
     * @return The bound methods.
     */
    static Bound bind(const ::krpc::Connection& connection);
  };

  /**
   * A client connected to the server.
   *
   * See krpc::services::KRPC::clients().
   */
  struct ClientInfo {
    /**
     * The identifier of the client.
     */
    std::string id;
    /**
     * The name of the client, or an empty string if it has no name.
     */
    std::string name;
    /**
     * The address of the client.
     */
    std::string address;
    /**
     * The protocol version of the client's connection.
     */
    uint32_t protocol_version;

    ClientInfo() : id(), name(), address(), protocol_version() {}

    ClientInfo(std::string id, std::string name, std::string address, uint32_t protocol_version) :
id(id), name(name), address(address), protocol_version(protocol_version) {}

    bool operator==(const ClientInfo& other) const {
      return this->id == other.id && this->name == other.name && this->address == other.address && this->protocol_version == other.protocol_version;
    }

    bool operator!=(const ClientInfo& other) const {
      return !(*this == other);
    }

    // Ordered by the fields in turn, which is how a tuple of the same values orders
    bool operator<(const ClientInfo& other) const {
      if (id < other.id)
        return true;
      if (other.id < id)
        return false;
      if (name < other.name)
        return true;
      if (other.name < name)
        return false;
      if (address < other.address)
        return true;
      if (other.address < address)
        return false;
      if (protocol_version < other.protocol_version)
        return true;
      return false;
    }

    bool operator>(const ClientInfo& other) const {
      return other < *this;
    }

    bool operator<=(const ClientInfo& other) const {
      return !(other < *this);
    }

    bool operator>=(const ClientInfo& other) const {
      return !(*this < other);
    }
  };

  /**
   * The settings that control how the server executes calls.
   *
   * See krpc::services::KRPC::ServerStatus::settings.
   */
  struct ServerSettings {
    /**
     * Whether the server executes one call per update.
     */
    bool one_rpc_per_update;
    /**
     * The current maximum time spent executing calls per update, in microseconds.
     */
    uint32_t max_time_per_update;
    /**
     * Whether the server adjusts krpc::services::KRPC::ServerSettings::max_time_per_update to hold the frame rate.
     */
    bool adaptive_rate_control;
    /**
     * Whether the server waits for the next call in an update.
     */
    bool blocking_recv;
    /**
     * The time the server waits for the next call, in microseconds.
     */
    uint32_t recv_timeout;
    /**
     * The longest time a client may hold the game on one physics tick, in microseconds.
     *
     * See krpc::services::KRPC::hold_tick().
     */
    uint32_t tick_hold_timeout;

    ServerSettings() : one_rpc_per_update(), max_time_per_update(), adaptive_rate_control(), blocking_recv(), recv_timeout(), tick_hold_timeout() {}

    ServerSettings(bool one_rpc_per_update, uint32_t max_time_per_update, bool adaptive_rate_control, bool blocking_recv, uint32_t recv_timeout, uint32_t tick_hold_timeout) :
one_rpc_per_update(one_rpc_per_update), max_time_per_update(max_time_per_update), adaptive_rate_control(adaptive_rate_control), blocking_recv(blocking_recv), recv_timeout(recv_timeout), tick_hold_timeout(tick_hold_timeout) {}

    bool operator==(const ServerSettings& other) const {
      return this->one_rpc_per_update == other.one_rpc_per_update && this->max_time_per_update == other.max_time_per_update && this->adaptive_rate_control == other.adaptive_rate_control && this->blocking_recv == other.blocking_recv && this->recv_timeout == other.recv_timeout && this->tick_hold_timeout == other.tick_hold_timeout;
    }

    bool operator!=(const ServerSettings& other) const {
      return !(*this == other);
    }

    // Ordered by the fields in turn, which is how a tuple of the same values orders
    bool operator<(const ServerSettings& other) const {
      if (one_rpc_per_update < other.one_rpc_per_update)
        return true;
      if (other.one_rpc_per_update < one_rpc_per_update)
        return false;
      if (max_time_per_update < other.max_time_per_update)
        return true;
      if (other.max_time_per_update < max_time_per_update)
        return false;
      if (adaptive_rate_control < other.adaptive_rate_control)
        return true;
      if (other.adaptive_rate_control < adaptive_rate_control)
        return false;
      if (blocking_recv < other.blocking_recv)
        return true;
      if (other.blocking_recv < blocking_recv)
        return false;
      if (recv_timeout < other.recv_timeout)
        return true;
      if (other.recv_timeout < recv_timeout)
        return false;
      if (tick_hold_timeout < other.tick_hold_timeout)
        return true;
      return false;
    }

    bool operator>(const ServerSettings& other) const {
      return other < *this;
    }

    bool operator<=(const ServerSettings& other) const {
      return !(other < *this);
    }

    bool operator>=(const ServerSettings& other) const {
      return !(*this < other);
    }
  };

  /**
   * The performance statistics of the server.
   *
   * See krpc::services::KRPC::ServerStatus::statistics.
   */
  struct ServerStatistics {
    /**
     * The number of bytes received from clients.
     */
    uint64_t bytes_read;
    /**
     * The number of bytes sent to clients.
     */
    uint64_t bytes_written;
    /**
     * The rate bytes are received from clients, in bytes per second.
     */
    float bytes_read_rate;
    /**
     * The rate bytes are sent to clients, in bytes per second.
     */
    float bytes_written_rate;
    /**
     * The number of calls executed.
     */
    uint64_t rpcs_executed;
    /**
     * The number of calls executed per second.
     */
    float rpc_rate;
    /**
     * The time spent receiving and executing calls per update, in seconds.
     */
    float time_per_rpc_update;
    /**
     * The time spent receiving calls per update, in seconds.
     */
    float poll_time_per_rpc_update;
    /**
     * The time spent executing calls per update, in seconds.
     */
    float exec_time_per_rpc_update;
    /**
     * The number of calls left for the next update, because the update ran out of time
     * or the call yielded.
     */
    uint32_t deferred_calls;
    /**
     * The number of open streams, events included.
     */
    uint32_t streams;
    /**
     * The number of stream evaluations, one per stream per update.
     */
    uint64_t stream_evaluations;
    /**
     * The number of stream evaluations per second.
     */
    float stream_evaluation_rate;
    /**
     * The number of stream updates sent to clients.
     */
    uint64_t stream_updates_sent;
    /**
     * The time spent updating streams per update, in seconds.
     */
    float time_per_stream_update;

    ServerStatistics() : bytes_read(), bytes_written(), bytes_read_rate(), bytes_written_rate(), rpcs_executed(), rpc_rate(), time_per_rpc_update(), poll_time_per_rpc_update(), exec_time_per_rpc_update(), deferred_calls(), streams(), stream_evaluations(), stream_evaluation_rate(), stream_updates_sent(), time_per_stream_update() {}

    ServerStatistics(uint64_t bytes_read, uint64_t bytes_written, float bytes_read_rate, float bytes_written_rate, uint64_t rpcs_executed, float rpc_rate, float time_per_rpc_update, float poll_time_per_rpc_update, float exec_time_per_rpc_update, uint32_t deferred_calls, uint32_t streams, uint64_t stream_evaluations, float stream_evaluation_rate, uint64_t stream_updates_sent, float time_per_stream_update) :
bytes_read(bytes_read), bytes_written(bytes_written), bytes_read_rate(bytes_read_rate), bytes_written_rate(bytes_written_rate), rpcs_executed(rpcs_executed), rpc_rate(rpc_rate), time_per_rpc_update(time_per_rpc_update), poll_time_per_rpc_update(poll_time_per_rpc_update), exec_time_per_rpc_update(exec_time_per_rpc_update), deferred_calls(deferred_calls), streams(streams), stream_evaluations(stream_evaluations), stream_evaluation_rate(stream_evaluation_rate), stream_updates_sent(stream_updates_sent), time_per_stream_update(time_per_stream_update) {}

    bool operator==(const ServerStatistics& other) const {
      return this->bytes_read == other.bytes_read && this->bytes_written == other.bytes_written && this->bytes_read_rate == other.bytes_read_rate && this->bytes_written_rate == other.bytes_written_rate && this->rpcs_executed == other.rpcs_executed && this->rpc_rate == other.rpc_rate && this->time_per_rpc_update == other.time_per_rpc_update && this->poll_time_per_rpc_update == other.poll_time_per_rpc_update && this->exec_time_per_rpc_update == other.exec_time_per_rpc_update && this->deferred_calls == other.deferred_calls && this->streams == other.streams && this->stream_evaluations == other.stream_evaluations && this->stream_evaluation_rate == other.stream_evaluation_rate && this->stream_updates_sent == other.stream_updates_sent && this->time_per_stream_update == other.time_per_stream_update;
    }

    bool operator!=(const ServerStatistics& other) const {
      return !(*this == other);
    }

    // Ordered by the fields in turn, which is how a tuple of the same values orders
    bool operator<(const ServerStatistics& other) const {
      if (bytes_read < other.bytes_read)
        return true;
      if (other.bytes_read < bytes_read)
        return false;
      if (bytes_written < other.bytes_written)
        return true;
      if (other.bytes_written < bytes_written)
        return false;
      if (bytes_read_rate < other.bytes_read_rate)
        return true;
      if (other.bytes_read_rate < bytes_read_rate)
        return false;
      if (bytes_written_rate < other.bytes_written_rate)
        return true;
      if (other.bytes_written_rate < bytes_written_rate)
        return false;
      if (rpcs_executed < other.rpcs_executed)
        return true;
      if (other.rpcs_executed < rpcs_executed)
        return false;
      if (rpc_rate < other.rpc_rate)
        return true;
      if (other.rpc_rate < rpc_rate)
        return false;
      if (time_per_rpc_update < other.time_per_rpc_update)
        return true;
      if (other.time_per_rpc_update < time_per_rpc_update)
        return false;
      if (poll_time_per_rpc_update < other.poll_time_per_rpc_update)
        return true;
      if (other.poll_time_per_rpc_update < poll_time_per_rpc_update)
        return false;
      if (exec_time_per_rpc_update < other.exec_time_per_rpc_update)
        return true;
      if (other.exec_time_per_rpc_update < exec_time_per_rpc_update)
        return false;
      if (deferred_calls < other.deferred_calls)
        return true;
      if (other.deferred_calls < deferred_calls)
        return false;
      if (streams < other.streams)
        return true;
      if (other.streams < streams)
        return false;
      if (stream_evaluations < other.stream_evaluations)
        return true;
      if (other.stream_evaluations < stream_evaluations)
        return false;
      if (stream_evaluation_rate < other.stream_evaluation_rate)
        return true;
      if (other.stream_evaluation_rate < stream_evaluation_rate)
        return false;
      if (stream_updates_sent < other.stream_updates_sent)
        return true;
      if (other.stream_updates_sent < stream_updates_sent)
        return false;
      if (time_per_stream_update < other.time_per_stream_update)
        return true;
      return false;
    }

    bool operator>(const ServerStatistics& other) const {
      return other < *this;
    }

    bool operator<=(const ServerStatistics& other) const {
      return !(other < *this);
    }

    bool operator>=(const ServerStatistics& other) const {
      return !(*this < other);
    }
  };

  /**
   * The status of the server.
   *
   * See krpc::services::KRPC::get_status().
   */
  struct ServerStatus {
    /**
     * The version of the server, or `unknown`.
     */
    std::string version;
    /**
     * The protocol version of the connection.
     */
    uint32_t protocol_version;
    /**
     * The settings of the server.
     */
    KRPC::ServerSettings settings;
    /**
     * The performance statistics of the server.
     */
    KRPC::ServerStatistics statistics;

    ServerStatus() : version(), protocol_version(), settings(), statistics() {}

    ServerStatus(std::string version, uint32_t protocol_version, KRPC::ServerSettings settings, KRPC::ServerStatistics statistics) :
version(version), protocol_version(protocol_version), settings(settings), statistics(statistics) {}

    bool operator==(const ServerStatus& other) const {
      return this->version == other.version && this->protocol_version == other.protocol_version && this->settings == other.settings && this->statistics == other.statistics;
    }

    bool operator!=(const ServerStatus& other) const {
      return !(*this == other);
    }

    // Ordered by the fields in turn, which is how a tuple of the same values orders
    bool operator<(const ServerStatus& other) const {
      if (version < other.version)
        return true;
      if (other.version < version)
        return false;
      if (protocol_version < other.protocol_version)
        return true;
      if (other.protocol_version < protocol_version)
        return false;
      if (settings < other.settings)
        return true;
      if (other.settings < settings)
        return false;
      if (statistics < other.statistics)
        return true;
      return false;
    }

    bool operator>(const ServerStatus& other) const {
      return other < *this;
    }

    bool operator<=(const ServerStatus& other) const {
      return !(other < *this);
    }

    bool operator>=(const ServerStatus& other) const {
      return !(*this < other);
    }
  };
};

}  // namespace services

namespace encoder {

  inline std::string encode(const services::KRPC::GameScene& value) {
    return krpc::encoder::encode(static_cast<int32_t>(value));
  }

  inline std::string encode(const services::KRPC::TypeCode& value) {
    return krpc::encoder::encode(static_cast<int32_t>(value));
  }

  // A structure is sent as the values of its fields in order, which is the same encoding as
  // a tuple of those values. See the note on the collection encoders for why the message it
  // is sent in is kept between calls.
  inline std::string encode(const services::KRPC::ClientInfo& value) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    structMessage.Clear();
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.id));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.name));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.address));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.protocol_version));
    return krpc::encoder::encode(structMessage);
  }

  // A structure is sent as the values of its fields in order, which is the same encoding as
  // a tuple of those values. See the note on the collection encoders for why the message it
  // is sent in is kept between calls.
  inline std::string encode(const services::KRPC::ServerSettings& value) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    structMessage.Clear();
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.one_rpc_per_update));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.max_time_per_update));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.adaptive_rate_control));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.blocking_recv));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.recv_timeout));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.tick_hold_timeout));
    return krpc::encoder::encode(structMessage);
  }

  // A structure is sent as the values of its fields in order, which is the same encoding as
  // a tuple of those values. See the note on the collection encoders for why the message it
  // is sent in is kept between calls.
  inline std::string encode(const services::KRPC::ServerStatistics& value) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    structMessage.Clear();
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.bytes_read));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.bytes_written));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.bytes_read_rate));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.bytes_written_rate));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.rpcs_executed));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.rpc_rate));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.time_per_rpc_update));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.poll_time_per_rpc_update));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.exec_time_per_rpc_update));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.deferred_calls));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.streams));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.stream_evaluations));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.stream_evaluation_rate));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.stream_updates_sent));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.time_per_stream_update));
    return krpc::encoder::encode(structMessage);
  }

  // A structure is sent as the values of its fields in order, which is the same encoding as
  // a tuple of those values. See the note on the collection encoders for why the message it
  // is sent in is kept between calls.
  inline std::string encode(const services::KRPC::ServerStatus& value) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    structMessage.Clear();
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.version));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.protocol_version));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.settings));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.statistics));
    return krpc::encoder::encode(structMessage);
  }

}  // namespace encoder

namespace decoder {

  inline void decode(services::KRPC::GameScene& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    int32_t x;
    decode(x, data, connection);
    value = static_cast<services::KRPC::GameScene>(x);
  }

  inline void decode(services::KRPC::TypeCode& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    int32_t x;
    decode(x, data, connection);
    value = static_cast<services::KRPC::TypeCode>(x);
  }

  // Fields are only ever appended to a structure, so a value from a newer server may carry
  // more items than this client knows about, and those are ignored.
  inline void decode(services::KRPC::ClientInfo& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    if (!structMessage.ParseFromString(data)) throw EncodingError("Failed to decode message");
    if (structMessage.items_size() < 4)
      throw EncodingError("Value for ClientInfo does not have all of its fields");
    decode_item(value.id, structMessage.items(0), connection);
    decode_item(value.name, structMessage.items(1), connection);
    decode_item(value.address, structMessage.items(2), connection);
    decode_item(value.protocol_version, structMessage.items(3), connection);
  }

  // Fields are only ever appended to a structure, so a value from a newer server may carry
  // more items than this client knows about, and those are ignored.
  inline void decode(services::KRPC::ServerSettings& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    if (!structMessage.ParseFromString(data)) throw EncodingError("Failed to decode message");
    if (structMessage.items_size() < 6)
      throw EncodingError("Value for ServerSettings does not have all of its fields");
    decode_item(value.one_rpc_per_update, structMessage.items(0), connection);
    decode_item(value.max_time_per_update, structMessage.items(1), connection);
    decode_item(value.adaptive_rate_control, structMessage.items(2), connection);
    decode_item(value.blocking_recv, structMessage.items(3), connection);
