    decode_item(value.recv_timeout, structMessage.items(4), connection);
    decode_item(value.tick_hold_timeout, structMessage.items(5), connection);
  }

  // Fields are only ever appended to a structure, so a value from a newer server may carry
  // more items than this client knows about, and those are ignored.
  inline void decode(services::KRPC::ServerStatistics& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    if (!structMessage.ParseFromString(data)) throw EncodingError("Failed to decode message");
    if (structMessage.items_size() < 15)
      throw EncodingError("Value for ServerStatistics does not have all of its fields");
    decode_item(value.bytes_read, structMessage.items(0), connection);
    decode_item(value.bytes_written, structMessage.items(1), connection);
    decode_item(value.bytes_read_rate, structMessage.items(2), connection);
    decode_item(value.bytes_written_rate, structMessage.items(3), connection);
    decode_item(value.rpcs_executed, structMessage.items(4), connection);
    decode_item(value.rpc_rate, structMessage.items(5), connection);
    decode_item(value.time_per_rpc_update, structMessage.items(6), connection);
    decode_item(value.poll_time_per_rpc_update, structMessage.items(7), connection);
    decode_item(value.exec_time_per_rpc_update, structMessage.items(8), connection);
    decode_item(value.deferred_calls, structMessage.items(9), connection);
    decode_item(value.streams, structMessage.items(10), connection);
    decode_item(value.stream_evaluations, structMessage.items(11), connection);
    decode_item(value.stream_evaluation_rate, structMessage.items(12), connection);
    decode_item(value.stream_updates_sent, structMessage.items(13), connection);
    decode_item(value.time_per_stream_update, structMessage.items(14), connection);
  }

  // Fields are only ever appended to a structure, so a value from a newer server may carry
  // more items than this client knows about, and those are ignored.
  inline void decode(services::KRPC::ServerStatus& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    if (!structMessage.ParseFromString(data)) throw EncodingError("Failed to decode message");
    if (structMessage.items_size() < 4)
      throw EncodingError("Value for ServerStatus does not have all of its fields");
    decode_item(value.version, structMessage.items(0), connection);
    decode_item(value.protocol_version, structMessage.items(1), connection);
    decode_item(value.settings, structMessage.items(2), connection);
    decode_item(value.statistics, structMessage.items(3), connection);
  }

}  // namespace decoder

inline std::size_t hash_value(const services::KRPC::ClientInfo& value) {
  std::size_t seed = 0;
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.id));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.name));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.address));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.protocol_version));
  return seed;
}

inline std::size_t hash_value(const services::KRPC::ServerSettings& value) {
  std::size_t seed = 0;
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.one_rpc_per_update));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.max_time_per_update));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.adaptive_rate_control));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.blocking_recv));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.recv_timeout));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.tick_hold_timeout));
  return seed;
}

inline std::size_t hash_value(const services::KRPC::ServerStatistics& value) {
  std::size_t seed = 0;
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.bytes_read));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.bytes_written));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.bytes_read_rate));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.bytes_written_rate));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.rpcs_executed));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.rpc_rate));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.time_per_rpc_update));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.poll_time_per_rpc_update));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.exec_time_per_rpc_update));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.deferred_calls));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.streams));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.stream_evaluations));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.stream_evaluation_rate));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.stream_updates_sent));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.time_per_stream_update));
  return seed;
}

inline std::size_t hash_value(const services::KRPC::ServerStatus& value) {
  std::size_t seed = 0;
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.version));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.protocol_version));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.settings));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.statistics));
  return seed;
}

// A collection encoder or decoder calls encode and decode on the type it contains
// without qualifying them. The ones above are declared after those templates, so
// argument dependent lookup is what reaches them. It searches the namespace that
// encloses the type, which is this one. These name the same functions here, as a
// second function with the same signature is ambiguous to a compiler that finds both.
namespace services {
  using krpc::encoder::encode;
  using krpc::decoder::decode;
  using krpc::hash_value;
}  // namespace services

namespace services {

inline KRPC::KRPC(const ::krpc::Connection& connection):
  RemoteService(connection) {
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "ArgumentException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw ArgumentException(msg); });
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "ArgumentNullException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw ArgumentNullException(msg); });
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "ArgumentOutOfRangeException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw ArgumentOutOfRangeException(msg); });
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "DivideByZeroException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw DivideByZeroException(msg); });
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "Error",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw Error(msg); });
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "IndexOutOfRangeException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw IndexOutOfRangeException(msg); });
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "InvalidOperationException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw InvalidOperationException(msg); });
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "KeyNotFoundException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw KeyNotFoundException(msg); });
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "NotSupportedException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw NotSupportedException(msg); });
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "NullReferenceException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw NullReferenceException(msg); });
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "ObjectDestroyedException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw ObjectDestroyedException(msg); });
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "ObjectNotLoadedException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw ObjectNotLoadedException(msg); });
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "OverflowException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw OverflowException(msg); });
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "StreamRemovedException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw StreamRemovedException(msg); });
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "TimeoutException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw TimeoutException(msg); });
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "UnsupportedSceneException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw UnsupportedSceneException(msg); });
}

inline std::string KRPC::get_client_id() {
  auto _data = this->_connection.invoke("KRPC", "GetClientID", {}, ::krpc::Connection::Returns::value);
  std::string _result{};
  if (!_data.is_null())
    decoder::decode(_result, _data.value(), &this->_connection);
  return _result;
}

inline std::string KRPC::get_client_name() {
  auto _data = this->_connection.invoke("KRPC", "GetClientName", {}, ::krpc::Connection::Returns::value);
  std::string _result{};
  if (!_data.is_null())
    decoder::decode(_result, _data.value(), &this->_connection);
  return _result;
}

inline KRPC::Expression::Expression():
  RemoteObject("KRPC::Expression") {}

inline KRPC::Expression::Expression(const ::krpc::Connection& connection, uint64_t id):
  RemoteObject(connection, "KRPC::Expression", id) {}

inline bool KRPC::Expression::has_return_type() {
  std::vector<krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(1);
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(*this));
  auto _data = this->_connection.invoke("KRPC", "Expression_get_HasReturnType", _args, ::krpc::Connection::Returns::value);
  bool _result{};
  if (!_data.is_null())
    decoder::decode(_result, _data.value(), &this->_connection);
  return _result;
}

inline std::vector<KRPC::Type> KRPC::Expression::parameter_types() {
  std::vector<krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(1);
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(*this));
  auto _data = this->_connection.invoke("KRPC", "Expression_get_ParameterTypes", _args, ::krpc::Connection::Returns::value);
  std::vector<KRPC::Type> _result{};
  if (!_data.is_null())
    decoder::decode(_result, _data.value(), &this->_connection);
  return _result;
}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Expression::return_type() {
  std::vector<krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(1);
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(*this));
  auto _data = this->_connection.invoke("KRPC", "Expression_get_ReturnType", _args, ::krpc::Connection::Returns::value);
  KRPC::Type _result{};
  if (!_data.is_null())
    decoder::decode(_result, _data.value(), &this->_connection);

  return ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Expression>(_connection, _connection.build_call("KRPC", "Expression_static_Any", _args));
}

inline ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Expression> KRPC::Expression::if_then_else_stream(::krpc::Connection& _connection, KRPC::Expression condition, KRPC::Expression body, KRPC::Expression else_body) {
  std::vector<krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(3);
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(condition));
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(body));
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(else_body));
  return ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Expression>(_connection, _connection.build_call("KRPC", "Expression_static_IfThenElse", _args));
}

inline ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Expression> KRPC::Expression::to_list_stream(::krpc::Connection& _connection, KRPC::Expression arg) {
  std::vector<krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(1);

inline ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall KRPC::Expression::try_catch_call(::krpc::Connection& _connection, KRPC::Expression body, std::string service, std::string name, std::optional<KRPC::Expression> message, KRPC::Expression handler) {
  std::vector<krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(5);
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(body));
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(service));
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(name));
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(message));
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(handler));
  return _connection.build_call("KRPC", "Expression_static_TryCatch", _args);
}

inline KRPC::Expression KRPC::Expression::Bound::string_trim_end(KRPC::Expression arg) const {
  return KRPC::Expression::string_trim_end(_connection, arg);
}

  return KRPC::Expression::string_trim_start(_connection, arg);
}

  return KRPC::Expression::subtract(_connection, arg0, arg1);
}

  return KRPC::Expression::sum(_connection, arg);
}

  return KRPC::Expression::switch_(_connection, value, cases, bodies);
}

  return KRPC::Expression::take(_connection, arg, count);
}

  return KRPC::Expression::throw_(_connection, service, name, message);

  return KRPC::Expression::zip(_connection, arg1, arg2, func);

inline KRPC::Expression::Bound KRPC::Expression::bind(const ::krpc::Connection& connection) {
  return Bound(connection);
}

inline ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall KRPC::Type::string_call(::krpc::Connection& _connection) {
  return _connection.build_call("KRPC", "Type_static_String");
}

inline ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall KRPC::Type::struct_type_call(::krpc::Connection& _connection, std::string service, std::string name) {
  std::vector<krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(2);
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(service));
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(name));
  return _connection.build_call("KRPC", "Type_static_StructType", _args);
}

inline ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall KRPC::Type::tuple_type_call(::krpc::Connection& _connection, std::vector<KRPC::Type> value_types) {
  std::vector<krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(1);
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(value_types));
  return _connection.build_call("KRPC", "Type_static_TupleType", _args);
}

inline ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall KRPC::Type::u_int_call(::krpc::Connection& _connection) {
  return _connection.build_call("KRPC", "Type_static_UInt");
}

inline ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall KRPC::Type::u_long_call(::krpc::Connection& _connection) {
  return _connection.build_call("KRPC", "Type_static_ULong");
}

inline KRPC::Type::Bound::Bound(const ::krpc::Connection& connection):
  _connection(connection) {}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Type::Bound::bool_() const {
  return KRPC::Type::bool_(_connection);
}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Type::Bound::bytes() const {
  return KRPC::Type::bytes(_connection);
}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Type::Bound::class_type(std::string service, std::string name) const {
  return KRPC::Type::class_type(_connection, service, name);
}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Type::Bound::dictionary_type(KRPC::Type key_type, KRPC::Type value_type) const {
  return KRPC::Type::dictionary_type(_connection, key_type, value_type);
}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Type::Bound::double_() const {
  return KRPC::Type::double_(_connection);
}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Type::Bound::enumeration_type(std::string service, std::string name) const {
  return KRPC::Type::enumeration_type(_connection, service, name);
}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Type::Bound::float_() const {
  return KRPC::Type::float_(_connection);
}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Type::Bound::int_() const {
  return KRPC::Type::int_(_connection);
}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Type::Bound::list_type(KRPC::Type value_type) const {
  return KRPC::Type::list_type(_connection, value_type);
}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Type::Bound::long_() const {
  return KRPC::Type::long_(_connection);
}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Type::Bound::nullable_type(KRPC::Type value_type) const {
  return KRPC::Type::nullable_type(_connection, value_type);
}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Type::Bound::set_type(KRPC::Type value_type) const {
  return KRPC::Type::set_type(_connection, value_type);
}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Type::Bound::string() const {
  return KRPC::Type::string(_connection);
}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Type::Bound::struct_type(std::string service, std::string name) const {
  return KRPC::Type::struct_type(_connection, service, name);
}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Type::Bound::tuple_type(std::vector<KRPC::Type> value_types) const {
  return KRPC::Type::tuple_type(_connection, value_types);
}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Type::Bound::u_int() const {
  return KRPC::Type::u_int(_connection);
}

inline KRPC::Type KRPC::Type::Bound::u_long() const {
  return KRPC::Type::u_long(_connection);
}

inline KRPC::Type::Bound KRPC::Type::bind(const ::krpc::Connection& connection) {
  return Bound(connection);
}
}  // namespace services

}  // namespace krpc

// A structure is a type of this program's own, so unlike a tuple it can be given a std::hash,
// and an unordered container of one needs nothing further.
namespace std {

template <>
struct hash<krpc::services::KRPC::ClientInfo> {
  std::size_t operator()(const krpc::services::KRPC::ClientInfo& value) const {
    return krpc::hash_value(value);
  }
};

template <>
struct hash<krpc::services::KRPC::ServerSettings> {
  std::size_t operator()(const krpc::services::KRPC::ServerSettings& value) const {
    return krpc::hash_value(value);
  }
};

template <>
struct hash<krpc::services::KRPC::ServerStatistics> {
  std::size_t operator()(const krpc::services::KRPC::ServerStatistics& value) const {
    return krpc::hash_value(value);
  }
};

template <>
struct hash<krpc::services::KRPC::ServerStatus> {
  std::size_t operator()(const krpc::services::KRPC::ServerStatus& value) const {
    return krpc::hash_value(value);
  }
};

}  // namespace std

#if defined(__GNUC__)
#pragma GCC diagnostic pop
#elif defined(_MSC_VER)
#pragma warning(pop)
#endif
